Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in general theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 431 and the invariant I(A,B) = 260 mod 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the general space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 431 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 260. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the general space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 647 and the convergence rate is O(126/n^5).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to general theory, we decompose the problem into 647 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 126. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
